# Amarakośa \[San\]

amarakoṣa evaṁ nāmaliṅgānuśāsanaṁ khāṇḍa 1 ..

śrīḥ\|\|

nāmaliṅgānuśāsanaṁ nāma amarakoṣaḥ \|

prathamaṁ kāṇḍam\|

maṅgalācaraṇam\|

(2.6.868) tyāgo vihāpitaṁ dānamutsarjanavisarjane

(2.6.869) viśrāṇanaṁ vitaraṇaṁ sparśanaṁ pratipādanam

(2.6.871) mrtārthaṁ tadahe dānaṁ triṣu syādaurdhvadehikam

(2.6.873) anvāhāryaṁ māsikeṁ'śo'ṣṭamo'hnaḥ kutapo'striyām

(2.6.876) ṣaṭtu triṣvarghyamarghārthe pādyaṁ pādāya vāriṇi

(2.6.877) kramādātithyātitheye atithyarthe'tra sādhuni

(2.6.878) syurāveśika āganturatithirnā rghāgate

(2.6.879) prāghūrṇikaḥ prāghūṇakaścābhyutthānaṁ tu gauravam

(2.6.880) pūjā namasyāpacitiḥ saparyārcārhaṇāḥ samāḥ

(2.6.888) paryāyaścātipātastu syātparyaya upātyayaḥ

(2.6.889) niyamo vratamastrī taccopavāsādi puṇyakam

(2.6.891) syādbrahmavarcasaṁ rvttādhyayanarddhirathāñjaliḥ

(2.6.893) dhyānayogāsane brahmāsanaṁ kalpe vidhikramau

(2.6.894) mukhyaḥ syātprathamaḥ kalpo'nukalpastu tato'dhamaḥ

(2.6.895) saṁskārapūrvaṁ grahaṇaṁ syādupākaraṇaṁ śruteḥ

(2.6.897) bhikṣuḥ parivrāṭ karmandī pārāśaryapi maskarī

(2.6.900) r ṣayaḥ satyavacasaḥ snātakastvāpluto vratī

(2.6.906) astrī kamaṇḍaluḥ kuṇḍī vratināmāsanaṁ rbṣī

(2.6.907) ajinaṁ carma rkttiḥ strī bhaikṣaṁ bhikṣākadambakam

(2.6.908) svādhyāyaḥ syājjapaḥ sutyābhiṣavaḥ savanaṁ ca sā

(2.6.910) darśaśca paurṇamāsaśca yāgau pakṣāntayoḥ rpthak

(2.6.912) niyamastu sa yatkarma nityamāgantusādhanam

(2.6.915) upavītaṁ brahmasūtraṁ prorddhte dakṣiṇe kare

(2.6.918) madhye'ṅguṣṭhāṅgulyoḥ pitryaṁ mūle tvaṅguṣṭhasya brāhmam

(2.6.922) naṣṭāgniḥ kuhanā lobhānmithyeryāpathakalpanā

(2.6.923) vrātyaḥ saṁskārahīnaḥ syādasvādhyāyo nirārktiḥ

(2.6.924) dharmadhvajī liṅgarvttiravakīrṇī kṣatavrataḥ

(2.6.925) supte yasminnastameti supte yasminnudeti ca

(2.6.926) aṁśumānabhinirmuktābhyuditau ca yathākramam

(2.6.928) parivittistu tajjāyānvivāhopayamau samau

(2.6.929) tathā pariṇayodvāhopayāmāḥ pāṇipīḍanam

(2.7.944) rakṣivargastvanīkastho'thādhyakṣādhirktau samau

(2.7.962) lipikāro'kṣaracaraṇo'kṣaracuñcuśca lekhake

(2.7.966) svāmyamātyasurhtkośarāṣṭradurgabalāni ca

(2.7.969) ṣaḍguṇā śaktayastisraḥ prabhāvotsāhamantrajāḥ

(2.7.970) kṣayaḥ sthānaṁ ca rvddhiśca trivargo nītivedinām

(2.7.972) bhedo daṇḍaḥ sāma dānamityupāyacatuṣṭayam

(2.7.973) sāhasaṁ tu samo (damo) daṇḍaḥ sāma sāntvamatho samau

(2.7.977) rahaścopāṁśu cāliṅge rahasyaṁ tadbhave triṣu

(2.7.981) nyāyyaṁ ca triṣu ṣaṭ sampradhāraṇā tu samarthanam

(2.7.985) āgo'parādho mantuśca same tūddānabandhane

(3.3.442) sthāṇuḥ śarvo'pyatha droṇaḥ kāke'pyājau rave raṇaḥ

(3.3.492) prakṛtir yoniliṅge ca kaiśikyā''dyāś ca vṛttayaḥ

(3.3.494) vanitā janitā'tyarthā'nurāgāyāṁ ca yoṣiti

(3.3.497) vāsitā strī kariṇyoś ca vārtā vṛttau janaśrutau

(3.3.500) śrutaṁ śāstrā'vadhṛtayor yugaparyāptayoḥ kṛtam

(3.3.501) atyāhitaṁ mahābhītiḥ karma jīvā'napekṣi ca

(3.3.528) abhiprāya vaśau chandāvabdau jīmūta vatsarau

(3.3.531) nirvādo janavāde'pi śādo jambāla śaṣpayoḥ

(3.3.532) ārāve rudite trātaryākrando dāruṇe raṇe

(3.3.533) syāt prasādo'nurage'pi sūdaḥ syād vyañjane'pi ca

(3.3.534) goṣṭhā'dhyakṣe'pi govindo harṣe'pyāmodavan madaḥ

(3.3.535) prādhānye rājaliṅge ca vṛṣāṅge kakudo'striyām

(3.3.539) goṣpadaṁ sevite māne pratiṣṭhā kṛtyamāspadam

(3.3.544) vyāmo vaṭaś ca nyagrodhāvutsedhaḥ kāya unnatiḥ

(3.3.545) paryāhāraś ca mārgaśh ca vivadhau vīvadhau ca tau

(3.3.546) paridhir yajñiya taroḥ śākhāyāmupasūryake

(3.3.551) vidhur viṣṇau candramasi paricchede bile'vadhiḥ

(3.3.552) vidhir vidhāne daive'pi praṇidhiḥ prārthane care

(3.3.554) deśe nada viśeṣe'bdhau sindhur nā sariti striyām

(3.3.555) vidhā vidhau prakāre ca sādhū ramye'pi ca triṣu

(3.3.562) leśe'pi gandhaḥ sambādho guhyasaṅkulayorapi

(3.3.564) sūrya vahnī citrabhānū bhānū raśmi divākarau

(3.3.565) bhūtātmānau dhātṛ dehau mūrkha nīcau pṛthagjanau

(3.3.566) grāvāṇau śailapāṣāṇau patriṇau śarapakṣiṇau

(3.3.567) taruśailau śikhariṇau śikhinau vahni barhiṇau

(3.3.568) pratiyatnāvubhau lipsopagrahāvatha sādinau

(3.3.569) dvau sārathi hayārohau vājino'śveṣu pakṣiṇaḥ

(3.3.570) kule'pyabhijano janma bhūmyāmapyatha hāyanāḥ

(3.3.571) varṣārcir vrīhibhedāś ca candrāgnyarkā virocanāḥ

(3.3.572) keśe'pi vṛjino viśvakarmā'rka suraśilpinoḥ

(3.3.573) ātmā yatno dhṛtir buddhiḥ svabhāvo brahma varṣma ca

(3.3.574) śakro ghātuka mattebho varṣukā'bdo ghanāghanaḥ

(3.3.575) abhimāno'rthā''di darpe jñāne praṇaya hiṁsayoḥ

(3.3.576) ghano meghe mūrtiguṇe triṣu mūrte nirantare

(3.3.577) inaḥ sūrye prabhau rājā mṛgāṅke kṣatriye nṛpe

(3.3.578) vāṇinyau nartakī dūtyau sravantyāmapi vāhinī

(3.3.579) hlādinyau vajrataḍitau vandāyāmapi kāminī

(3.3.580) tvag dehayorapi tanuḥ sūnā'dho jihvikā'pi ca

(3.3.581) kratu vistārayorastrī vitānaṁ triṣu tucchake

(3.3.582) mande'tha ketanaṁ kṛtye ketāvupanimantraṇe

(3.3.583) vedas tattvaṁ tapo brahma brahmā vipraḥ prajāpatiḥ

(3.3.584) utsāhane ca hiṁsāyāṁ sūcane cā'pi gandhanam

(3.3.585) ātañcanaṁ pratīvāpa javanā''pyāyanā'rthakam

(3.3.586) vyañjanaṁ lāñchanaṁ śmaśru niṣṭhānā'vayaveṣvapi

(3.3.587) syāt kaulīnaṁ lokavāde yuddhe paśvahi pakṣiṇām

(3.3.588) syādudyānaṁ niḥsaraṇe vanabhede prayojane

(3.3.589) avakāśe sthitau sthānaṁ krīḍā''dāvapi devanam

(3.3.590) vyutthānaṁ pratirodhe ca virodhā''caraṇe'pi ca

(3.3.591) utthānaṁ pauruṣe tantre sanniviṣṭodgame'pi ca

(3.3.592) māraṇe mṛtasaṁskāre gatau dravye'rtha dāpane

(3.3.593) nirvartanopakaraṇā'nuvrajyāsu ca sādhanam

(3.3.594) niryātanaṁ vaira śuddhau dāne nyāsā'rpaṇe'pi ca

(3.3.595) vyasanaṁ vipadi bhraṁśe doṣe kāmajakopaje

(3.3.596) pakṣmā'kṣilomni kiñjalke tantvā''dyamśe'pyaṇīyasi

(3.3.602) gṛhadehatviṭprabhāvā dhāmānyatha catuṣpathe

(3.3.641) naigamau dvau bale rāmo nīlacārusite triṣu

(3.3.644) uṣṇe'pi gharmaś ceṣṭā'laṅkāre bhrāntau ca vibhramaḥ

(3.3.676) chāyā sūryapriyā kāntiḥ pratibimbamanātapaḥ

(3.3.679) janyaḥ syājjanavāde'pi jaghanyo'ntye'dhame'pi ca

(3.3.681) ātmavānanapeto'rthādarthyau puṇyaṁ tu cārvapi

(3.3.695) vedabhede guptavāde mantro mitro ravāvapi

(3.3.697) āḍambaras tūryarave gajendrāṇāṁ ca garjite

(3.3.698) abhihāro'bhiyoge ca caurye sannahane'pi ca

(3.3.699) syājjaṅgame parīvāraḥ khaḍgakośe paricchade

(3.3.702) vipule nakule viṣṇau babhrurnā piṅgale triṣu

(3.3.703) sāro bale sthirā'ṁśe ca nyāyye klībaṁ vare triṣu

(3.3.704) durodaro dyūtakāre paṇe dyūte durodaram

(3.3.705) mahā'raṇye durgapathe kāntāraṁ punnapuṁsakam

(3.3.706) matsaro'nyaśubhadveṣe tadvatkṛpaṇayostriṣu

(3.3.707) devādvṛte varaḥ śreṣṭhe triṣu klībaṁ manākpriye

(3.3.708) vaṁśāṅkure karīro'strī tarubhede ghaṭe ca nā

(3.3.709) nā camūjaghane hastasūtre pratisaro'striyām

(3.3.710) yamā'nilendracandrārkaviṣṇusiṁhā'ṁśuvājiṣu

(3.3.712) śarkarā karparā'ṁśe'pi yātrā syādyāpane gatau

(3.3.713) irā bhūvāksurā'psusyāt tandrī nidrāpramīlayoḥ

(3.3.714) dhātrī syādupamātā'pi kṣitirapyāmalakyapi

(3.3.715) kṣudrā vyaṅgā naṭī veśyā saraghā kaṇṭakārikā

(3.3.717) alpe ca parimāṇe sā mātraṁ kārtsnye'vadhāraṇe

(3.3.719) yogyabhājanayoḥ pātraṁ patraṁ vāhanapakṣayoḥ

(3.3.724) ajiraṁ viṣaye kāye'pyambaraṁ vyomni vāsasi

(3.3.762) abdhyambuvikṛtau velā kālamaryādayorapi

(3.3.764) līlā vilāsakriyayorupalā śarkarā'pi ca

(3.3.765) śoṇite'mbhasi kīlālaṁ mūlamādye śiphābhayoḥ

(3.3.766) jālaṁ samūha ānāyagavākṣakṣārakeṣvapi

(3.3.768) chadirnetrarujoḥ klībaṁ samūhe paṭalaṁ na nā

(3.3.785) bhāvaḥ sattāsvabhāvā'bhiprāyaceṣṭā''tmajanmasu

(3.3.788) utsekā'marṣayoricchāprasare maha utsavaḥ

(3.3.789) anubhāvaḥ prabhāve ca satāṁ ca matiniścaye

(3.3.821) syādvṛṣṭau lokadhātvaṁśe vatsare varṣamastriyām

(3.3.822) prekṣā nṛttekṣaṇaṁ prajñā bhikṣā sevā'rthanā bhṛtiḥ

(3.3.823) tviṭ śobhā'pi triṣu pare nyakṣaṁ kārtsnyanikṛṣṭayoḥ
